Timing

Solved cases: 6.7 s in total, median 0.0603 s per case, over 110 solved case(s) with a time. 2 answers took at least 60% of their query's timeout, marked ⌛ below: a re-run may time out. Times are wall-clock seconds per query as the runner measured them (each query in its own forked process; setup and warm-up not timed); totals and medians are over the solved cases only, so a timeout, error or no verdict never counts as a fast answer. They are shown next to the counts, never combined with them.

Measured on machine pi5 · indicative: it has some other load: times are a fair guide, large differences between its runs mean something, small ones may be noise. Raspberry Pi 5, 4 cores. Benchmark runs go one at a time, in a low-priority systemd unit (CPUWeight=20, Nice=10); since 2026-10-17, each eval is pinned to core 3 (earlier runs were not pinned). The Pi also serves websites, and other work may share core 3 unless it is reserved for the runs (pi5-deploy INSTALL.md, step 15). Clock frequency and temperature are not controlled.

// weakened: (!= x (- i)) (!= of a complex variable (Giac stores real intervals only); dropped)
// weakened: (nonzero x) (!= of a complex variable (Giac stores real intervals only); dropped)
complex_mode:=1;
try { assume(x, complex); aeOut("FACT|0|OK|assume(x, complex)"); } catch (aeErr) { aeOut("FACT|0|ERROR|" + string(aeErr)); };
aeOut("STEP|about(x)|OK|" + string(about(x)));
aeOk := 0;
